No type is redefined across crates; cross-crate use is via orc-api/orc-store re-exports only. Adding a new kind = crates/orc-api/src/<kind>.rs from templates/new-kind.rs.tmpl + register in lib.rs GVK map.

11. Core Schema and Core Types

Normative type surface for all nine orc-* crates. Signatures are LOCKED; bodies shown are illustrative (≤2 lines). Cross-cutting laws apply to every type below.

11.0 Cross-cutting type laws

Law Statement
Versioned-replace Objects are NOT mutated in place in orc-store. A write produces a NEW KeyValue at a higher Revision; old bytes survive until compact. In-memory &mut Object builds the next version, then put replaces.
resourceVersion == mod_rev ObjectMeta.resource_version is the decimal string of the store KeyValue.mod_rev at last write. Opaque to clients; compared for equality only, never arithmetic.
Single durable truth Only orc-store persists. All caches (SharedIndexInformer) are derived + reconstructible by relist+watch.
Serialization JSON via serde; TypeMeta flattened into each object (apiVersion,kind at top level). Field-order irrelevant; codec is canonical for fuzz round-trip (Phase 14).
Concurrency Store serializes writes by Revision (one global monotonic logical clock). Read paths are Arc-shared, lock-free for readers. Reconcilers run concurrently, keyed by object (namespace,name) — at most one in-flight reconcile per key (WorkQueue dedup).
Compatibility apiVersion gates schema; evolution is ADDITIVE (new optional fields, Option<_>/default). Removing/retyping a field requires a new apiVersion. Unknown fields preserved on round-trip (no silent drop).

11.1 orc-api — object model + apimachinery

/// GroupVersionKind discriminant, flattened into every object's JSON.
pub struct TypeMeta { pub api_version: String, pub kind: String } // e.g. "v1","Pod"

/// Identity + lineage + lifecycle metadata shared by ALL kinds.
pub struct ObjectMeta {
    pub name: String,                       // unique within (namespace, kind)
    pub namespace: Option<String>,          // None => cluster-scoped (Node, Namespace)
    pub uid: Uuid,                           // server-assigned, immutable, identity across name reuse
    pub resource_version: String,           // == store mod_rev; opaque; equality-compare only
    pub generation: i64,                     // bumped on spec change; status echoes observedGeneration
    pub labels: BTreeMap<String,String>,     // selector key space; BTree => deterministic JSON/hash
    pub annotations: BTreeMap<String,String>,// non-selectable metadata
    pub owner_references: Vec<OwnerReference>,// GC + adoption lineage
    pub finalizers: Vec<String>,             // block deletion until drained
    pub creation_timestamp: Time,
    pub deletion_timestamp: Option<Time>,    // Some => terminating; object still readable until finalizers clear
}

/// Every kind implements Object => generic Registry/Informer over `dyn Object`.
pub trait Object {
    fn type_meta(&self) -> &TypeMeta;
    fn meta(&self) -> &ObjectMeta;
    fn meta_mut(&mut self) -> &mut ObjectMeta;
}
  • Ownership: each object owned by the store as bytes; in-memory copies owned by whoever decoded them. No shared mutable object — copy, edit, CAS-write.
  • Mutation: meta_mut only for client-side construction of the next version; never aliased while a write is in flight.
  • uid immutable for the object's life; reusing name after delete yields a NEW uid (prevents stale-owner adoption).
  • generation advanced by apiserver ONLY on spec mutation; controllers compare observedGeneration (in status) to detect un-acted spec changes.
pub struct Pod    { pub meta: ObjectMeta, pub spec: PodSpec, pub status: PodStatus }

pub struct PodSpec {
    pub containers: Vec<Container>,          // >=1; validated non-empty
    pub volumes: Vec<Volume>,
    pub node_name: Option<String>,           // None => unscheduled; set EXACTLY ONCE by Bind
    pub restart_policy: RestartPolicy,       // Always | OnFailure | Never
    pub node_selector: BTreeMap<String,String>,
    pub tolerations: Vec<Toleration>,
    pub affinity: Option<Affinity>,          // node/pod (anti)affinity rules
}

pub struct PodStatus {
    pub phase: PodPhase,
    pub conditions: Vec<Condition>,          // PodScheduled, Ready, ContainersReady, Initialized
    pub container_statuses: Vec<ContainerStatus>,
    pub pod_ip: Option<IpAddr>,              // assigned by NetworkPlugin
}

pub enum PodPhase { Pending, Running, Succeeded, Failed, Unknown }
  • Single-writer split: scheduler/controllers write spec; the bound node's orc-kubelet is the SOLE writer of status. Prevents status races without locks.
  • node_name: write-once (Bind). A second bind attempt for a non-empty node_name is a 409/invalid — binding is exclusive.
  • PodPhase terminal set = {Succeeded, Failed}; Unknown => node unreachable (lease lost). Phase transitions are monotone except via delete+recreate.
pub struct ReplicaSet { pub meta: ObjectMeta, pub spec: ReplicaSetSpec, pub status: ReplicaSetStatus }
pub struct ReplicaSetSpec {
    pub replicas: i32,                       // desired N
    pub selector: LabelSelector,             // MUST match template labels (validated)
    pub template: PodTemplateSpec,           // pod stamp; mutating template != rolling (Deployment's job)
}
pub struct ReplicaSetStatus { pub replicas: i32, pub ready_replicas: i32, pub observed_generation: i64 }

/// spec.strategy: RollingUpdate{max_surge, max_unavailable} | Recreate.
/// Owns a sequence of ReplicaSets (one per pod-template revision) via OwnerReferences.
pub struct Deployment { pub meta: ObjectMeta, pub spec: DeploymentSpec, pub status: DeploymentStatus }

/// spec.selector -> EndpointSlice; spec.ports; spec.cluster_ip (VIP, alloc'd from CIDR);
/// spec.type: ClusterIP | NodePort.
pub struct Service { pub meta: ObjectMeta, pub spec: ServiceSpec, pub status: ServiceStatus }

/// Cluster-scoped. status.capacity / allocatable (cpu, memory, pods);
/// status.conditions (Ready, MemoryPressure, DiskPressure); spec.taints.
pub struct Node { pub meta: ObjectMeta, pub spec: NodeSpec, pub status: NodeStatus }
  • ReplicaSet.selector immutable post-create (re-selection would orphan pods); enforced by validation.
  • Deployment never owns Pods directly — it owns ReplicaSets; ReplicaSets own Pods. Two-level OwnerReference chain drives cascading GC.
/// Conjunction of equality labels AND set-based expressions. Empty selector matches ALL.
pub struct LabelSelector {
    pub match_labels: BTreeMap<String,String>,
    pub match_expressions: Vec<Requirement>, // op: In | NotIn | Exists | DoesNotExist
}

/// Lineage edge: child -> owner. controller=true marks the single managing controller.
pub struct OwnerReference {
    pub uid: Uuid,                           // owner identity; stale uid => orphan (owner gone)
    pub kind: String,
    pub controller: bool,                    // exactly one controller=true owner per child
    pub block_owner_deletion: bool,          // owner delete blocked until child cleared (finalizer)
}

/// Standard status condition (mirrors k8s metav1.Condition).
pub struct Condition {
    pub type_: String, pub status: ConditionStatus, // True|False|Unknown
    pub reason: String, pub message: String,
    pub last_transition_time: Time, pub observed_generation: i64,
}
  • LabelSelector evaluation is pure + total over a label map; used by ReplicaSet adopt/orphan, Service endpoint selection, scheduler affinity. Selector match must be O(labels) — index by label in the informer for reverse lookup.

11.2 orc-store — MVCC KV store

pub struct Revision(pub i64);              // global monotonic logical clock; 0 = "before any write"

pub struct KeyValue {
    pub key: Vec<u8>, pub value: Vec<u8>,  // value = JSON-encoded object bytes
    pub create_rev: Revision,              // rev of first Put for this key (since last delete)
    pub mod_rev: Revision,                 // rev of last Put == object.resource_version
    pub version: i64,                      // per-key write count since create (1-based)
    pub lease: Option<LeaseId>,            // Some => key auto-deleted when lease expires
}

pub enum WatchEvent {
    Put(KeyValue, Option<KeyValue>),       // new + prev (None on create)
    Delete(KeyValue),                      // the tombstoned kv (last value)
    Bookmark(Revision),                    // progress marker; no data change up to this rev
}
  • MVCC: every Put/Delete bumps the global Revision; the keyspace retains historical versions until compact(rev). range(..., rev=Some(r)) is a consistent snapshot read AS OF r.
  • mod_rev is the bridge to resourceVersion — the apiserver stamps meta.resource_version = mod_rev.to_string() on read.
pub trait Store {
    /// Range scan [key, end) at optional historical rev; returns kvs + current store rev (for watch start).
    async fn range(&self, key: &[u8], end: &[u8], rev: Option<Revision>) -> Result<RangeResult, StoreError>;
    /// Blind write; returns the new global Revision. Use txn() for CAS.
    async fn put(&self, key: &[u8], val: &[u8], lease: Option<LeaseId>) -> Result<Revision, StoreError>;
    /// Delete [key,end); tombstone bumps Revision; returns it.
    async fn delete(&self, key: &[u8], end: &[u8]) -> Result<Revision, StoreError>;
    /// Atomic compare-and-set: if all `compares` hold, apply on_ok else on_else. Basis of optimistic concurrency.
    async fn txn(&self, compares: Vec<Compare>, on_ok: Vec<Op>, on_else: Vec<Op>) -> Result<TxnResult, StoreError>;
    /// Ordered, gap-free, dup-free stream of WatchEvent with rev > start_rev; 410-Expired if start_rev compacted.
    fn watch(&self, key: &[u8], end: &[u8], start_rev: Revision) -> WatchStream;
    /// TTL lease; keys attached to it die on expiry. Used for Node heartbeats.
    async fn lease_grant(&self, ttl: Duration) -> Result<LeaseId, StoreError>;
    async fn lease_keepalive(&self, id: LeaseId) -> Result<(), StoreError>;
    /// Drop history <= rev; subsequent watch(start_rev<=rev) -> 410. Reclaims space.
    async fn compact(&self, rev: Revision) -> Result<(), StoreError>;
}

pub struct Compare { pub key: Vec<u8>, pub target: CompareTarget } // Value | ModRevision | CreateRevision | Version
pub enum Op { Put { key: Vec<u8>, val: Vec<u8>, lease: Option<LeaseId> }, Delete { key: Vec<u8> }, Range { key: Vec<u8> } }

pub struct Lease { pub id: LeaseId, pub ttl: Duration, pub granted_at: Time, pub attached: Vec<Vec<u8>> }
  • Concurrency: writes are serialized through a single append point (WAL) → total Revision order. Reads + watches are lock-free against a snapshot. txn is the ONLY atomic multi-key primitive.
  • Optimistic concurrency law: apiserver update = txn([Compare{key, ModRevision == observed_rv}], [Put], []); on !succeeded → 409 Conflict, controller re-reads + retries. No locks anywhere.
  • Watch gap-freedom is the cache-correctness foundation: events strictly ordered by mod_rev, none skipped, none repeated; Bookmark lets a watcher advance its known rev without a data event so re-watch resumes correctly.
  • Lease semantics: a missed lease_keepalive past ttl auto-deletes attached keys → emits Delete watch events → Node-lifecycle controller reacts. Single mechanism for liveness.
  • Persistence: WAL is the durable record; in-memory index (key → version list) is rebuilt by WAL replay on restart. Raft replication hook (cross-link 42) wraps the WAL append for HA; deferred.

11.3 orc-controller — reconciliation framework

/// Level-triggered, IDEMPOTENT. Reads CURRENT desired+observed from cache, drives toward desired.
/// Never acts on an event payload; the Request carries only the object KEY.
pub trait Reconciler {
    async fn reconcile(&self, req: Request, ctx: &Context) -> Result<Reconcile, ReconcileError>;
}
pub enum Reconcile { Done, RequeueAfter(Duration), Requeue } // requeue feeds the rate limiter

pub struct Request { pub namespace: Option<String>, pub name: String } // identity only — re-read from Lister

/// Reflector(list+watch) -> DeltaFIFO -> applies to a thread-safe indexed cache + fans out to handlers.
/// Shared: many controllers attach to ONE informer per kind (single watch connection).
pub struct SharedIndexInformer<T> { /* reflector + delta FIFO + Arc<RwLock<indexed cache>> + indexers */ }

/// Read-only typed view over the informer cache. Cheap clones via Arc; never blocks the watch loop.
pub struct Lister<T> { /* fn get(ns,name) -> Option<Arc<T>>; fn list(selector) -> Vec<Arc<T>> */ }

/// Rate-limited, de-duplicated, optionally delayed work items. At most one in-flight per key.
pub struct WorkQueue<K> { /* add / add_after / add_rate_limited / get -> K / done(K) / forget(K) */ }

/// Per-key backoff schedule (e.g. exponential 5ms..1000s) for failed reconciles.
pub trait RateLimiter<K> {
    fn when(&self, item: &K) -> Duration; // delay before next attempt
    fn forget(&self, item: &K);           // reset backoff on success
}
  • Ownership: the SharedIndexInformer owns the authoritative in-memory cache (Arc<RwLock<...>>); Listers hand out Arc<T> snapshots — readers never mutate, never block the watch apply loop.
  • Concurrency: WorkQueue dedup guarantees a key is processed by ONE worker at a time even if enqueued N times; reconciles for DISTINCT keys run in parallel (worker pool). Missed/coalesced events are harmless (level-triggered re-read).
  • resync periodically re-enqueues all cached keys → defends against any dropped watch event; convergence is eventual regardless of event loss.
  • Reconcile::RequeueAfter schedules a delayed retry WITHOUT consulting the rate limiter; Requeue + errors go THROUGH the rate limiter.

11.4 orc-scheduler — scheduling framework

/// Per-pod scheduling cycle state: the pod, a node Lister, the scheduler cache, and plugin scratch state.
pub struct SchedulingContext<'a> { /* pod: &Pod, nodes: &Lister<Node>, cache: &Cache, state: PluginState */ }

/// Hard predicate: is this Node feasible for this Pod? Any reject => Node excluded.
pub trait FilterPlugin {
    fn filter(&self, pod: &Pod, node: &Node, ctx: &SchedulingContext) -> FilterResult; // Pass | Reject(reason)
}
/// Soft priority: rank a feasible Node 0..100. Plugin scores are weighted-summed.
pub trait ScorePlugin {
    fn score(&self, pod: &Pod, node: &Node, ctx: &SchedulingContext) -> i64; // 0..=100
}
/// Result of a cycle: the write that assigns the Pod to a Node.
pub struct Binding { pub pod: ObjectRef, pub node: String }
  • Algorithm: Filter all Nodes → feasible set; Score each feasible Node (Σ weightᵢ·scoreᵢ); pick argmax (ties broken deterministically); Binding → apiserver writes pod.spec.node_name.
  • Exclusivity: Bind is a CAS on node_name == None; concurrent schedulers / double-schedule collapse to one winner (409 for the loser).
  • Concurrency: built-in Filter/Score plugins are PURE functions of (pod,node,snapshot) → parallelizable across nodes; SchedulingContext is borrowed for the cycle, not stored.

11.5 orc-kubelet — node agent

/// One PodWorker per Pod bound to this Node; owns that Pod's lifecycle state machine end-to-end.
pub struct PodWorker { /* pod uid, desired spec, last status, runtime handle, sync channel */ }

/// Outcome of one sync; drives the kubelet's per-pod loop.
pub enum PodSyncResult {
    Synced(PodStatus),     // converged; report this status to the apiserver
    Backoff(Duration),     // container crash -> retry after backoff
    Terminated,            // pod fully stopped + cleaned up
}

/// Liveness / readiness / startup probing.
pub trait ProbeManager {
    async fn probe(&self, p: &Probe, c: &ContainerStatus) -> ProbeResult; // Success | Failure | Unknown
}
  • Single status writer: PodWorker computes PodStatus and is the ONLY writer of that Pod's status to the apiserver (one-writer-per-field rule).
  • Ownership/concurrency: one PodWorker task per pod-uid; workers are independent (no shared mutable pod state) → pod syncs run concurrently across the node.
  • Probes drive conditions: readiness failure → Ready=False → EndpointSlice removes the pod IP (no traffic); liveness failure → restart per restart_policy with Backoff; startup gates the others until first success.

11.6 orc-runtime — container runtime (CRI-like)

pub trait ContainerRuntime {
    /// Create the pod sandbox (shared net/IPC namespaces + pause-equivalent); returns its id.
    async fn run_sandbox(&self, pod: &PodSandboxConfig) -> Result<SandboxId, RuntimeError>;
    /// Pull + verify + unpack an OCI image into the ImageStore; idempotent on digest.
    async fn pull_image(&self, image: &ImageRef, auth: Option<&Auth>) -> Result<ImageId, RuntimeError>;
    /// Create (not start) a container in an existing sandbox from an unpacked image.
    async fn create_container(&self, sb: SandboxId, c: &ContainerConfig) -> Result<ContainerId, RuntimeError>;
    async fn start_container(&self, id: ContainerId) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>;
    /// Stop with grace period (SIGTERM then SIGKILL at deadline).
    async fn stop_container(&self, id: ContainerId, grace: Duration) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>;
    async fn status(&self, id: ContainerId) -> Result<ContainerStatus, RuntimeError>;
}

/// OCI runtime config: root fs, process, namespaces, cgroups v2 path, mounts, capabilities.
pub struct OciSpec { /* root, process(args,env,cwd,caps), linux{namespaces, resources(cgroups v2), mounts}, hooks */ }

/// A running pod sandbox: shared namespaces + the assigned PodIp; containers join it.
pub struct Sandbox { pub id: SandboxId, pub netns: NetnsHandle, pub pod_ip: Option<PodIp>, pub cgroup: CgroupHandle }

/// Content-addressed image storage: digest -> unpacked rootfs layers + config.
pub struct ImageStore { /* layer cache keyed by sha256 digest; ref-counted; gc on unused */ }
  • Linux-real / stub split: on Linux, start_container clones into namespaces + applies cgroups v2 + pivot_root; on other OSes a stub runtime satisfies the trait for dev/test.
  • Idempotency: pull_image keyed by digest (no-op if present); run_sandbox/create_container keyed by pod-uid + container name so a re-sync after kubelet restart adopts existing objects, not duplicates.
  • Trust boundary: image bytes + workload are UNTRUSTED to the node; isolation (namespaces, cgroups, dropped caps, read-only rootfs) is the containment. OciSpec is the security-critical surface — validated before exec.
  • CgroupHandle owns the cgroup v2 path for a sandbox; resource limits (cpu/memory from container requests/limits) applied here; handle drop reclaims the cgroup.

11.7 orc-net — service networking

/// Programs the dataplane: Service ClusterIP VIP -> healthy EndpointSlice backends (+ NodePort).
pub struct ServiceProxy { /* watches Service + EndpointSlice; reprograms rules on change */ }

/// Pod-network lifecycle (CNI-like): attach/detach a Sandbox to the cluster pod network.
pub trait NetworkPlugin {
    async fn setup_pod_network(&self, sb: &Sandbox) -> Result<PodIp, NetError>;  // alloc IP + wire netns
    async fn teardown_pod_network(&self, sb: &Sandbox) -> Result<(), NetError>;  // release IP + cleanup
}
  • Level-triggered dataplane: ServiceProxy reconciles the FULL desired rule set from current Service + EndpointSlice cache (not per-event deltas) → resilient to missed watch events; idempotent reprogram.
  • Only Ready-condition endpoints become backends; readiness-gated by kubelet probes (§11.5) → no traffic to unready pods.
  • setup_pod_network is called by the kubelet AFTER run_sandbox, BEFORE container start; returned PodIp flows into Sandbox.pod_ip and ultimately PodStatus.pod_ip (written by the single status writer).

12. Phase 1 — Bootstrap, object-model shells, and orc-store MVCC key-value store

Goal: Set up the nine-crate Cargo workspace, lay down object-model shells and the all-in-one dev binary (bootstrap/smoke-test), then build the single durable source of truth: a WAL-backed MVCC key-value store with gap-free watch, leases, and optimistic concurrency — every control-plane invariant starts here.

1.1 Objectives

  • Bootstrap: stand up the Cargo workspace (edition 2021, MSRV 1.78, Tokio); lay compile-only shells for every cross-crate boundary (orc-api object model, orc-store Store trait, orc-controller Reconciler/SharedIndexInformer, orc-scheduler FilterPlugin/ScorePlugin, orc-runtime ContainerRuntime); build the orc-allinone dev binary; pass the smoke test (POST a Pod → persist → GET returns it with server-assigned uid + resourceVersion); establish no-panic typed-error policy; green CI.
  • orc-store: WAL-backed keyspace with one monotonic global Revision (i64, +1 per committed mutation) as logical clock ↔ resourceVersion; ops: range/get, put, delete, txn (compare-and-set), watch (gap-free, Revision-ordered, Bookmarks), lease_grant/keepalive (TTL), compact; crash-recover via WAL replay; MVCC retention; cascade-delete leased keys on lease expiry; single-node only, raft_hook.rs seam for later HA (cross-link 42).

1.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-01–03 (Rust/Tokio, workspace), ADR-04/05 (build orc-store from scratch, WAL+BTree engine), ADR-07/08 (typed structs, serde/JSON), ADR-09/10/11 (Revision=RV, optimistic-CC, gap-free watch), ADR-28 (error model); INV-1 (Revision monotonic), INV-2 (watch gap-freedom), INV-12 (crash-consistent WAL); §5.1 Rust for Rustaceans (async trait, seams), DDIA ch.3/7 (MVCC, WAL), OSTEP (crash recovery); §5.2 etcd MVCC/Watch/Lease docs, Raft paper; §5.4 Kubernetes API Conventions.

Workspace + shells (bootstrap):

Module / file Adds
Cargo.toml (root) [workspace] members=[orc-api,orc-store,orc-apiserver,orc-controller,orc-scheduler,orc-kubelet,orc-runtime,orc-net,orctl]; resolver="2"; shared [workspace.dependencies]
orc-api/src/{meta,pod,replicaset,service,node,object,selector,codec}.rs TypeMeta, ObjectMeta, trait Object, Pod/PodSpec/PodStatus/PodPhase, ReplicaSet/Service/Node shells, LabelSelector, OwnerReference; serde JSON codec
orc-store/src/{lib,kv,watch,lease,txn,memory}.rs Revision(i64), KeyValue, WatchEvent, trait Store (all 8 async methods); MemStore (BTreeMap-backed, smoke-path only)
orc-apiserver/src/{registry,handlers,server,bin}.rs Registry<T:Object> over Store; axum routes POST/GET /api/v1/namespaces/{ns}/pods; assign uid, resource_version, creation_timestamp on POST
orc-controller/src/{informer,reconciler,workqueue}.rs trait Reconciler, enum Reconcile, Request, SharedIndexInformer<T>, WorkQueue<K>, trait RateLimiter<K> — signatures + todo() bodies
orc-scheduler, orc-kubelet, orc-runtime, orc-net, orctl FilterPlugin/ScorePlugin, PodWorker/PodSyncResult/ProbeManager, ContainerRuntime, ServiceProxy/NetworkPlugin, typed REST client — all shells
orc-allinone binary tokio::main → MemStore::new() → serve apiserver on :8080; docker-compose.yml (1 apiserver + 2 node skeletons)

orc-store full implementation (crates/orc-store/src/):

File Contents
wal.rs append-only segment log; record = crc32 ‖ len ‖ rev ‖ payload; fsync before ACK; segment rotation @ 64 MiB; replay scan (torn tail = bad CRC → discarded)
mvcc.rs apply committed entries to BTree index + value store; range(rev) reads snapshot at rev
txn.rs evaluate compares → branch on_ok/on_else; single global write lock serializes commits; one Revision per txn
watch.rs WatchStream; per-key/range watcher registry; replay from start_rev then live tail; Bookmark timer; bounded per-watcher channel (slow → 410)
lease.rs LeaseId; min-heap by expiry; keepalive resets; expiry task cascades delete of attached keys
compact.rs drop history < rev; mark compact_rev; range/watch below it → 410 Gone
raft_hook.rs trait ReplicationHook { async fn propose(&self, entry: &WalRecord) -> Result<Revision>; }; default NoopHook commits locally
  • Write path: put/txn → acquire write lock → assign rev=next() → WAL append + fsync → apply to MVCC index → publish WatchEvent to matching watchers → release lock → return rev.
  • Compare variants: Value, ModRevision, CreateRevision, Version, Exists/NotExists; op ∈ {=,≠,<,>}. CAS = ModRevision(key, =, rv).
  • Watch replay: if start_rev > compact_rev, serve buffered history then attach live; ordered by (rev, key).
  • Error policy: every crate exposes thiserror *Error; control-loop code returns Result, never panic!/unwrap/expect (clippy disallowed-methods lint enforces on non-test code).

1.3 Deliverable

  • Smoke: cargo run -p orc-apiserver --bin orc-allinone serves REST on :8080; make smoke POSTs pod.json, GETs it, asserts round-trip + assigned uid/resourceVersion. Artifacts: Makefile, Dockerfile, docker-compose.yml, .github/workflows/ci.yml.
  • Store: orc-store crate with WAL-backed MemStore impl of Store; passes watch-gap-freedom, CAS-conflict, lease-expiry, crash-replay tests. orc-apiserver (Phase 3) links it directly; no network surface yet.

1.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
cargo build --workspace clean, all 9 crates
cargo fmt --check + cargo clippy -- -D warnings zero; panic/unwrap/expect in non-test src = 0
Smoke round-trip POST 201 → GET 200, metadata.uid non-nil v4, resourceVersion parses as i64 > 0, spec byte-identical
Revisions strictly increasing no gaps/reuse across 1e6 mixed ops; mod_rev == rev of last write
Watch gap-freedom watch from R delivers every event with rev>R exactly once in (rev,key) order; verified by diff vs op log (0 gaps, 0 dups)
CAS exclusivity concurrent txn on same key → exactly one commits; loser sees on_else; never both
Lease expiry TTL expiry deletes all attached keys within ≤ttl+1s; keepalive before expiry retains
WAL crash-recovery kill -9 after fsync → replay yields identical committed prefix; partial trailing record (bad CRC) discarded
Performance POST p50 < 5 ms, GET p50 < 2 ms (smoke/loopback); store put p99 < 200 µs, range 1k keys < 2 ms (local SSD)

1.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
fixtures/pod.json valid Pod POST 201, GET equal-after-strip(uid,rv,ts)
fixtures/pod_no_name.json missing metadata.name 422 Unprocessable, store not hit
fixtures/pod_bad_kind.json kind:Frob 400 Bad Request
fixtures/store/revision_monotonic.json seq put/del/txn rev sequence 1..N, no gap
fixtures/store/watch_gapfree.json concurrent puts + watch@R event set == ops with rev>R, sorted
fixtures/store/cas_conflict.json two txn ModRevision(k,=,5) one succeeded:true, one false+on_else
fixtures/store/lease_expiry.json grant ttl=2s, attach k, sleep 3s k gone, WatchEvent::Delete emitted
fixtures/store/compact_410.json compact@10, watch@5 error Compacted{compact_rev:10} (410)
fixtures/store/wal_replay/*.wal golden WAL incl. torn-tail replay → fixed snapshot hash; torn record dropped

1.6 Pitfalls

  • Watch gap on concurrent write: publish outside the write lock → later rev arrives before earlier. Fix: enqueue under lock. Debug: assert per-watcher last_rev strictly increases.
  • History/live race: watcher attaches after reading history snapshot but misses gap events. Fix: register live subscription before draining history, dedup by mod_rev.
  • fsync skipped: apiserver acks a write that survives only in RAM. Fix: fsync (or group-commit) before returning rev. Debug: kill -9 harness.
  • Lease keepalive lost under load: coarse timer tick. Fix: timer wheel keyed to next expiry.
  • resourceVersion read before put commits (smoke): reading Revision separately from the put. Fix: put returns assigned Revision atomically.
  • serde deny_unknown_fields omitted: typo fields silently dropped. Set on all object types from day 0.

1.7 Performance Concerns

  • Single global write lock = commit bottleneck; mitigate with group-commit (batch WAL fsync, one rev each) targeting > 50k put/s.
  • Watch fan-out O(watchers·matched); index watchers by key-prefix interval tree for 10k concurrent streams.
  • MVCC index memory: Vec<KeyIndex> per key grows unbounded without compaction; auto-compact every 1000 revs.
  • In-memory MemStore smoke target: POST p50 < 5 ms, GET p50 < 2 ms (no fsync for smoke only).

1.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty body POST → 400. Duplicate POST same name → 409 AlreadyExists. GET nonexistent → 404. resourceVersion supplied by client on create → ignored (server-owned).
  • Empty range / end < key; put same value → still new rev + version++ (idempotency is caller's job).
  • Delete non-existent key in txn on_ok: no-op, txn still commits. Lease attached to key, then key overwritten without lease: detaches.
  • Watch start_rev = 0 → "from now"; start_rev = current → next event only. Compact at already-compacted rev → idempotent no-op.

1.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Torn WAL tail (crash mid-fsync) CRC fail on last record drop record, replay prefix; committed prefix consistent
Disk full on WAL append ENOSPC → StoreError::Io reject write, return error to apiserver (no partial rev)
Corrupt mid-WAL record CRC fail not at tail fail-stop: refuse start, require operator (data loss boundary)
Lease expiry task starved watchdog: max heap-head age log + force-drain heap; alert
store put errors mid-request Result propagated 500 + typed StoreError, no partial object visible
port :8080 bound bind error at startup exit non-zero with clear msg, not hang

1.10 Operational Notes

  • Smoke run: cargo run -p orc-apiserver --bin orc-allinone; docker compose up for apiserver + 2 node skeletons. Flags: --listen, --store=memory; RUST_LOG/tracing env for structured logs.
  • Store metrics: store_revision (gauge), store_put_latency/txn_latency (hist), store_watchers, store_wal_fsync_seconds, store_compact_rev, lease_count.
  • WAL dir + value store path configurable; segment size 64 MiB; retain ≥ 2 segments post-compaction. compact exposed via apiserver maint endpoint; auto-compact interval tunable. raft_hook.rs NoopHook is the only commit path in Phase 1; HA deferred to Phase 13 / project 42.
  • CI matrix: linux MSRV 1.78 + stable; macOS stable (stub runtime). Health: GET /healthz → 200 once store + listener ready.

13. Phase 2 — orc-api: object model, selectors, codec, resourceVersion

2.1 Objectives

  • Define orc-api as the apimachinery foundation: TypeMeta, ObjectMeta, Object trait, all typed kinds → every other crate depends only on orc-api for object shapes.
  • LabelSelector matching (matchLabels + matchExpressions: In/NotIn/Exists/DoesNotExist) + field selectors; one canonical matcher used by scheduler/controller/apiserver.
  • JSON codec (serde) with apiVersion/kind tagging, lossless round-trip, unknown-field policy → wire compatibility across components.
  • resourceVersion semantics: meta.resource_version == store mod_rev decimal string; opaque/ordered; never client-authored. generation bumps on spec change only.
  • ListOptions (label+field selector, resourceVersion, limit/continue) → pagination + consistent-read contract for apiserver/informer.

2.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-07 (typed structs, exhaustive match), ADR-08 (serde/JSON codec), ADR-09 (resourceVersion = Revision), ADR-10 (optimistic-CC fields); P-6 (single-writer-per-field on spec vs status); INV-3 (resourceVersion CAS prerequisite — field exists here); §5.4 Kubernetes API Conventions (TypeMeta/ObjectMeta, GVK, conditions, status subresource); §5.3 kubernetes/apimachinery (field-name parity reference).

File (orc-api/src/) Contents
meta.rs TypeMeta, ObjectMeta, Object trait (type_meta/meta/meta_mut), OwnerReference, Condition, Time(RFC3339), Uuid newtype
kinds/{pod,replicaset,deployment,service,node,namespace,configmap,secret,event,endpointslice}.rs spec/status structs per LOCKED sigs; enum PodPhase, RestartPolicy, Service.spec.type, Deployment.spec.strategy
selector.rs LabelSelector, Requirement{key,op:Operator,values}, fn matches(&self, labels:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->bool
fields.rs FieldSelector — registered paths per kind (metadata.name, metadata.namespace, spec.nodeName, status.phase); Eq/NotEq only
codec.rs encode(&dyn Object)->Vec<u8> / decode(&[u8])->Box<dyn Object>; #[serde(tag="kind")]-style dispatch via apiVersion+kind; deny_unknown_fields toggle per ListOptions strictness
list.rs ListOptions{label_selector,field_selector,resource_version:Option<String>,limit:u32,continue_token:Option<String>}, List<T>{meta:ListMeta{resource_version,continue},items:Vec<T>}, WatchEvent<T>{Added,Modified,Deleted,Bookmark}
  • Algorithms: selector match = AND over matchLabels ∪ matchExpressions; codec uses serde_json with deterministic key order (BTreeMap) for stable bytes; generation increment computed by apiserver (Phase 3), orc-api only carries the field.
  • Defaulting + validation hooks: fn validate(&self)->Result<(),Vec<ValidationError>> per kind (name DNS-1123, selector⊇template.labels for ReplicaSet) — pure functions, no I/O.

2.3 Deliverable

orc-api crate (std, no tokio): all kinds encode/decode round-trip byte-identical; matches() + field-selector evaluator; ListOptions/List/WatchEvent; per-kind validate(). Unit + conformance tests green; cargo test -p orc-api passes.

2.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Round-trip: for every kind, decode(encode(x)) == x (PartialEq), byte-stable across runs (deterministic key order).
  • Selector: conformance table (≥40 cases) all pass; matches() < 1 µs for ≤16 labels.
  • Unknown field rejected under strict mode (apiserver create), tolerated under non-strict (watch decode of newer server).
  • resourceVersion is opaque string; encoding never parses it as int in orc-api; ordering left to orc-store.
  • generation field present, defaults 1; validate() rejects empty name, non-DNS-1123, selector/template mismatch.

2.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
fixtures/kinds/*.json (one valid per kind) decode→re-encode byte-identical
fixtures/selector_table.json (selector, labels, expect:bool) matcher output == expect for all rows
fixtures/field_selector.json path eval == expect; unknown path → ErrUnknownField
fixtures/malformed/missing_kind.json DecodeError::MissingTypeMeta
fixtures/malformed/unknown_field_strict.json DecodeError::UnknownField{path}
fixtures/malformed/bad_phase.json (phase:"Booting") DecodeError::InvalidEnum{field:"status.phase"}
fixtures/malformed/rv_as_int.json accepted (string), but int form → type error

2.6 Pitfalls

  • Non-deterministic JSON key order (HashMap) → bytes differ run-to-run → false 409s downstream. Root cause: HashMap iteration. Fix: BTreeMap everywhere; debug via sha256(encode(x)) stability test.
  • Treating resourceVersion as integer in orc-api → leaks store internals, breaks opacity contract; large RVs overflow if parsed as i32. Keep String; only orc-store interprets.
  • matchExpressions In with empty values → must be invalid (validation error), not match-all; root cause: silent default. Debug: assert non-empty for In/NotIn.
  • Exists vs In confusion: DoesNotExist requires empty values; mismatch → spurious matches. Validate at decode.
  • Omitting creation_timestamp/uid on client-submitted objects is correct (server-set); rejecting them as missing → cannot apply. Treat as Option/server-populated.
  • #[serde(flatten)] on TypeMeta interacts badly with deny_unknown_fields → false unknown-field errors. Use manual tag extraction in codec.rs.

2.7 Performance Concerns

  • matches(): pre-sort requirements, short-circuit on first miss; target <1 µs/16-label object → enables informer-side selector filtering at 10k objects.
  • Codec: serde_json from-slice; avoid intermediate Value (typed deserialize); encode reuses a Vec<u8> buffer pool in hot list paths.
  • ObjectMeta clone cost: labels/annotations BTreeMap dominate; share via Arc in informer cache (Phase 4), not in orc-api.
  • List encode of 10k Pods < 50 ms; per-object < 5 µs encode.

2.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty LabelSelector ({}) matches ALL objects; nil/None selector matches NOTHING — distinct, must not conflate.
  • Duplicate keys across matchLabels and matchExpressions → AND, both must hold.
  • namespace: None (cluster-scoped: Node, Namespace, ClusterRole) vs Some("") → normalize None; reject "".
  • Label value "" is valid; In:[""] matches it; Exists matches key-present-any-value.
  • Unicode/long label values: value ≤63 chars, key ≤253 (prefix/name) per DNS rules — validate() enforces.
  • Secret.data base64; round-trip must not decode/re-encode lossily (preserve exact bytes).

2.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Mitigation
Codec drift between server/client versions round-trip byte test + version-tagged fixtures additive-only fields; non-strict decode for forward compat
Selector false-positive adopts wrong Pods conformance table + property test (random labels) exact AND semantics; validate empty-values In/NotIn
resourceVersion parsed/mutated in orc-api grep guard test (no parse::<i64> on RV) keep opaque String; ordering only in orc-store
Enum widening breaks old decoders bad_phase fixture; #[non_exhaustive] enums tolerate-unknown variant under non-strict

2.10 Operational Notes

  • Version skew: orc-api is the shared schema crate; bump apiVersion (e.g. v1→v1beta2) additively; old clients non-strict-decode.
  • Debug: orctl get -o json emits exact orc-api codec bytes → diffable against fixtures.
  • Metrics surfaced later (Phase 3): apiserver_request_decode_errors_total{kind,reason} keyed by DecodeError variants here.
  • No I/O, no async, no tokio in orc-api → unit-testable in isolation, links into all six consumer crates without runtime deps.

14. Phase 3 — orc-apiserver: REST CRUD, admission, namespaces, watch endpoint, optimistic concurrency

3.1 Objectives

  • Generic Registry<T: Object> over orc-store: kind ⇄ /registry/<kind>/<ns>/<name> key mapping; one codepath for all kinds.
  • REST handlers: create / get / list / update / patch / delete; namespaced vs cluster-scoped kinds; namespace existence + finalizer-on-delete gating.
  • Admission trait: mutating then validating chain; per-resource validation; defaulting.
  • WATCH endpoint: HTTP chunked stream of WatchEvent from a resourceVersion, Bookmarks, 410-Gone relist contract.
  • Optimistic concurrency: update carries read resourceVersion; server Txn CAS on store Revision; mismatch → 409.
  • authn/authz hook points (no-op pass-through now; filled Phase 12). NON-GOAL: RBAC matrix, webhooks (Phase 12).

3.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-10 (optimistic-CC: Txn CAS → 409), ADR-09 (resourceVersion = mod_rev), ADR-12 (HTTP/1.1 chunked watch transport), ADR-07 (typed registry per kind), ADR-08 (JSON wire format); INV-3 (CAS rejects stale writes); P-2 (store single source of truth), P-3 (no locks); §5.4 Kubernetes API Conventions (create/update/delete verb semantics, status subresource, resourceVersion opacity); §5.3 pkg/registry (Generic Registry strategy pattern).

Module (orc-apiserver/src/) Adds
registry.rs Registry<T> { store, kind, scope }; key(ns,name), prefix(ns); create/get/list/update/patch/delete over Store.
handlers.rs axum routes /{apiVersion}/namespaces/{ns}/{plural}[/{name}] + cluster-scoped /{apiVersion}/{plural}; verb dispatch.
admission.rs Admission trait; AdmissionChain (mutating Vec → validating Vec); built-ins: NamespaceLifecycle, default-injection.
watch.rs watch_handler: Store::watch(prefix, start_rev) → Transfer-Encoding: chunked newline-delimited JSON WatchEvent; bookmark ticker (default 60s).
scope.rs Scope::{Namespaced,Cluster} per kind; reject ns on cluster-scoped (422).
server.rs (Phase 1) wire router; authn middleware Authenticator + authz Authorizer (allow-all stubs).
  • key(ns,name) → /registry/{kind}/{ns}/{name}; cluster-scoped uses sentinel ns "". List = range(prefix, prefix_end, rev).
  • Admission trait:
pub trait Admission {
    async fn admit(&self, req: &mut AdmissionRequest) -> Result<(), AdmissionError>; // mutating: edits obj; validating: read-only
    fn kind(&self) -> AdmissionKind; // Mutating | Validating
}
  • Create: authn→authz→default→mutate→validate→assign uid+creation_timestamp→Txn(compare key absent, put); 409 if key exists.
  • Update: read RV from body ObjectMeta.resource_version; Txn([Compare::ModRev(key,==,rv)], [put], []); on !succeeded → 409. Bump generation iff spec changed.
  • resource_version of stored object = decimal string of KeyValue.mod_rev; emitted on every read/list/watch.
  • Delete: if finalizers non-empty → set deletion_timestamp, leave object (foreground GC, Phase 5); else Txn delete with RV-CAS.

3.3 Deliverable

orc-apiserver binary serving full CRUD + list + watch over the Phase-2 object model, persisted via orc-store; orctl get/apply round-trips; admission chain wired; 409 + watch-from-RV + admission-reject tests pass.

3.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Create→Get round-trips object byte-identical except server-set uid/resourceVersion/creationTimestamp.
  • Stale-RV update → HTTP 409 Conflict with reason: "Conflict"; fresh re-read + retry succeeds.
  • Watch from RV R delivers every change with Revision > R in order, no gaps/dupes; Bookmark ≤ every 60s.
  • Watch from compacted RV → HTTP 410 Gone; client relists + re-watches at returned RV.
  • Create-into-missing-namespace → 404; rejected-by-validating-admission → 422 with cause list.
  • p99 create+watch-deliver latency < 50 ms at 1k concurrent watchers.

3.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expected
fixtures/apiserver/create_get_roundtrip.json POST Pod → 201; GET → 200, equal mod fields.
fixtures/apiserver/conflict_409.json two updates same RV → 1×200, 1×409.
fixtures/apiserver/watch_from_rv.ndjson ordered WatchEvent stream + Bookmark.
fixtures/apiserver/watch_compacted_410.json watch stale RV → 410 + relist RV.
fixtures/apiserver/admission_reject.json empty containers → 422, cause spec.containers: Required.
fixtures/apiserver/cluster_scoped_ns_422.json ns path on Node kind → 422.
Malformed unknown kind → 404; bad JSON → 400; non-numeric RV → 400; PATCH unknown field (strict) → 422.

3.6 Pitfalls

  • Read-modify-write without CAS → lost update; ALWAYS Txn(ModRev==rv). Debug: diff mod_rev vs sent RV in audit log.
  • List resourceVersion ≠ max child RV → use the store range's snapshot Revision as the list RV (watch resumes there); using max child RV drops concurrent deletes. Symptom: watcher misses a delete after relist.
  • Bookmark gap: forget bookmarks on idle keyspace → client RV staleness → spurious 410 on resume. Emit even when no events.
  • Mutating admission after RV-CAS computed → object mutated post-validation; order MUST be mutate→validate→CAS.
  • Watch chunk buffering by reverse-proxy → client sees no events; set Cache-Control: no-cache, flush per event.
  • Patch merge semantics: strategic-merge vs JSON-merge confusion → list fields clobbered. Pick JSON-merge (RFC 7386) Phase-3; strategic-merge deferred.

3.7 Performance Concerns

  • Single-flight watch fan-out: one Store::watch per prefix, broadcast to N HTTP streams (don't open N store watches). Target 10k watchers, < 200 MB apiserver RSS.
  • List pagination: ListOptions.limit + continue token = base64({key, rev}); avoids O(all) materialization.
  • JSON codec on hot path: reuse serde_json buffers; target < 30 µs encode per Pod.
  • Watch event broadcast: bounded per-client channel (cap 1000); slow client → close with 410, not back-pressure the store.

3.8 Edge Cases

  • Concurrent create same name → exactly one 201, other 409 (Txn key-absent compare).
  • Delete already-deleted → 404 (idempotent client treats as success).
  • Update changing metadata.name/uid/namespace → 422 (immutable fields).
  • resourceVersion: "0" on list = "any cached version, fastest"; on watch = "from now".
  • Empty list → 200 {items: []} with current list RV (not 404).
  • Watch across compaction mid-stream → emit final events then 410.

3.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Mitigation
Store unavailable on write Txn returns StoreError 503 Retry-After; do not partial-write.
Watch stream stalls (slow client) per-client send timeout close stream → client relists.
Admission webhook timeout (Phase 12 hook) deadline on admit fail-closed for validating, fail-open configurable for mutating.
RV monotonic violation (store bug) assert mod_rev increases refuse serve, alert; gap-freedom invariant broken.
Orphaned finalizer (no GC) object stuck Terminating Phase-5 GC; manual finalizer removal escape hatch.

3.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: apiserver_request_duration by verb/kind/code; apiserver_watchers gauge; apiserver_409_total; apiserver_410_total.
  • Audit log: every write { verb, kind, ns/name, user-stub, sent RV, result RV, code }.
  • Flags: --bind, --store-endpoint, --watch-bookmark-interval=60s, --max-request-bytes=1.5Mi, --list-limit-default=500.
  • Health: /healthz (process), /readyz (store reachable + initial range OK).
  • orctl get --watch consumes the chunked endpoint; verify with curl -N (no buffering).

15. Phase 4 — orc-controller framework: reflector, informer, workqueue, reconciler

4.1 Objectives

  • Generic controller runtime in orc-controller: Reflector → DeltaFIFO → SharedIndexInformer<T> (indexed shared cache + Lister) → rate-limited dedup WorkQueue<K> → Reconciler.
  • Enforce level-triggered contract: reconcile reads CURRENT state from cache, NEVER the event payload; idempotent; missed-event-harmless.
  • Periodic resync (re-enqueue all cache keys), 410 Gone relist, exponential-backoff RateLimiter, per-item forget, leader-election hook over a store Lease.

4.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-13 (Reflector→DeltaFIFO→SharedIndexInformer→WorkQueue), ADR-14 (rate-limited dedup queue), ADR-15 (level-triggered reconcile); INV-2 (watch gap-freedom: 410→relist path), INV-4 (reconcile idempotent); P-1 (level-triggered), P-5 (idempotent); §5.1 Programming Kubernetes ch.1–3 (pipeline shape), §5.3 client-go/tools/cache (Reflector, DeltaFIFO, SharedIndexInformer, workqueue); §5.2 "Level Triggering and Reconciliation" (reconcile-from-cache rule).

  • Files: orc-controller/src/reflector.rs, delta_fifo.rs, informer.rs, lister.rs, workqueue.rs, ratelimiter.rs, reconciler.rs, controller.rs, leaderelection.rs.
  • Reflector<T>: list(ListOptions) → seed cache @ rv0; watch(start_rv=rv0) stream; on each event push Delta{Added|Updated|Deleted|Sync, obj} into DeltaFIFO; on 410 Gone/Expired → relist + Replace (synthesize deletes for keys absent from relist) → re-watch from new rv. Bookmark advances last_rv.
  • DeltaFIFO: key→VecDeque<Delta> + FIFO key order; pop coalesces (drop Added+Deleted→nothing; collapse consecutive Updated); Replace(items, rv) for relist.
  • SharedIndexInformer<T>: drain FIFO → mutate ThreadSafeStore (HashMap<Key, T> under RwLock + indices HashMap<IndexName, HashMap<IndexValue, HashSet<Key>>>); fan out ResourceEventHandler{on_add,on_update(old,new),on_delete}; Lister::{get(key), list(), list_by_index(name,val)}.
  • WorkQueue<K>: add(K) (dedup via dirty: HashSet<K> + processing: HashSet<K>), add_after(K, Duration) (delay heap → ready set), get()→(K, shutdown), done(K) (re-add if marked dirty while processing), forget(K), shutdown().
  • RateLimiter<K> default = ExponentialBackoff{base=5ms, max=1000s, factor=2} × failures[K]; forget resets count.
  • controller.rs: spawn N reconcile workers; each loop{ (k,_)=q.get(); match r.reconcile(Request{k}, ctx){ Ok(Done)=>q.forget+done, Ok(RequeueAfter(d))=>q.forget+done+add_after(d), Ok(Requeue)|Err=>q.add_after(rl.when(k)); done } }.
  • leaderelection.rs: lease_grant(ttl=15s) + txn CAS on holder; lease_keepalive every 5s; on loss → cancel CancellationToken → workers stop.

4.3 Deliverable

  • Crate orc-controller exporting SharedIndexInformer, Lister, WorkQueue, RateLimiter, Reconciler, Reconcile, Request, Controller, LeaderElector. Example no-op reconciler binary driven by live orc-apiserver watch. cargo test -p orc-controller green.

4.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Watch-driven enqueue: POST/UPDATE/DELETE an object → key appears in WorkQueue ≤ 50 ms; reconcile invoked from cache state.
  • Dedup: 1000 rapid updates to one key while reconcile in-flight → ≤ 2 reconciles (in-flight + 1 dirty).
  • Backoff: failing reconcile re-enqueues at 5ms,10ms,20ms… capped 1000s; forget resets to 5ms.
  • Missed-event-harmless: drop one watch event → resync (≤ 30s) re-enqueues key → cache converges to store.
  • Leader election: 3 controllers, exactly 1 active; kill holder → another acquires ≤ ttl+keepalive (≤ 20s).

4.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Input Expected
fifo_coalesce.json Added,Updated,Deleted same key pop yields nothing (net no-op)
relist_410.json watch err 410 @ rv relist + synthetic Delete for absent keys, re-watch new rv
backoff_schedule.json 6 consecutive failures delays [5,10,20,40,80,160]ms
dedup_burst.json 1000 adds, 1 in-flight ≤2 dequeues
index_pods_by_node.json pods w/ node labels list_by_index("node","n1") = exact key set
Malformed non-monotonic rv in watch reject, force relist; never apply out-of-order

4.6 Pitfalls

  • Reconcile on event payload (edge-triggered) → stale/torn decisions; root cause: reading Delta.obj not Lister::get. Debug: assert reconcile ignores payload; fuzz-drop events.
  • done() without re-add when dirty → lost update, stuck until resync. Root cause: missing dirty∩processing re-queue. Debug: log dirty/processing set deltas.
  • Relist without synthetic deletes → ghost keys linger in cache → GC/RS act on dead objects. Debug: diff cache vs relist set.
  • RateLimiter not reset on success → unbounded backoff for flaky-then-healthy items. Debug: assert forget on Done.
  • Resync re-enqueues all keys at once → thundering herd. Mitigate: jittered per-key resync.
  • Watch from compacted rv loops 410→relist→410. Root cause: relist rv already compacted; must use relist's returned rv, not cached last_rv.

4.7 Performance Concerns

  • Targets: cache read Lister::get < 10 µs (RwLock read); watch→enqueue p99 < 50 ms; 10k-object informer steady RAM < 80 MB; resync 10k keys < 1 s.
  • RwLock read-mostly cache; avoid clone on get (return Arc<T>). WorkQueue ops O(1) (HashSet); delay via binary heap O(log n). FIFO coalescing bounds queue depth under bursts.

4.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty relist (all deleted) → synthesize deletes for every cached key.
  • Object deleted then recreated same name, new uid → treat as delete+add (uid change), not update.
  • Bookmark-only stream (no changes) → last_rv still advances → relist after compaction stays cheap.
  • Reconcile for key absent from cache (deleted) → Lister::get→None → reconcile handles deletion/no-op.
  • Concurrent add + shutdown → get() returns shutdown=true, drains no new items.

4.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
apiserver watch drops stream EOF/err reconnect from last_rv; 410→relist
Store compaction mid-watch 410 Gone relist @ fresh rv + Replace
Reconciler panic worker catch_unwind log, re-add via backoff, worker survives
Leader lease lost (partition) keepalive fail CancellationToken → stop reconciling; no split-brain
FIFO unbounded growth depth metric backpressure reflector; alert

4.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: workqueue_depth, workqueue_adds_total, reconcile_duration_seconds{result}, reconcile_errors_total, informer_cache_size, watch_relists_total, leader_is_active.
  • Logs: per-reconcile {kind,namespace,name,rv,result,duration}; relist + 410 at WARN.
  • Tunables: workers (default 2), resync period (30s, jittered), backoff base/max, lease ttl/renew (15s/5s).
  • Leader transition emits Event; non-leaders idle but keep informers warm for fast failover (< 20s).

16. Phase 5 — Core controllers: ReplicaSet, GarbageCollector, Node-lifecycle

5.1 Objectives

  • Implement three built-in Reconcilers on the Phase-4 framework (SharedIndexInformer + WorkQueue), all level-triggered + idempotent (read cache, drive observed → desired).
  • ReplicaSet: maintain spec.replicas Pods matching spec.selector via adopt/orphan through controller OwnerReference; create/delete Pods; write status.{replicas,readyReplicas,observedGeneration}.
  • GarbageCollector: build cluster-wide ownerRef dependency graph; cascading delete of children on owner delete; honor finalizers + blockOwnerDeletion; foreground/background/orphan policies.
  • Node-lifecycle: watch Node Lease; missed lease → Node Ready=False (NotReady); after grace → taint node.orc/unreachable:NoExecute + evict Pods so they reschedule.
  • Non-goal: Deployment/ReplicaSet orchestration (Phase 9), scheduling (Phase 6), PDB-aware drain (Phase 13).

5.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-15 (level-triggered reconcile), ADR-16 (OwnerReferences + finalizers + GC), ADR-13 (shared informer cache per type); INV-4 (reconcile convergent), INV-7 (no orphan children); P-1 (level-triggered), P-5 (idempotent), P-8 (ownership drives lifecycle); §5.1 Programming Kubernetes ch.6 (controller pattern), §5.2 "GC/finalizers" design proposal; §5.3 client-go/tools/cache (Lister), kubernetes/kubernetes GarbageCollector graph.

Module/file Content
orc-controller/src/controllers/replicaset.rs ReplicaSetReconciler{ pod_lister, rs_lister, client }; reconcile() per RS key
orc-controller/src/controllers/gc.rs GarbageCollector{ graph: OwnerGraph, queues }; attempt_to_delete + attempt_to_orphan
orc-controller/src/controllers/node_lifecycle.rs NodeLifecycleReconciler{ lease_lister, node_lister, grace }
orc-controller/src/controllers/ownergraph.rs OwnerGraph (uid → node{owners, deps, beingDeleted}); built from all-kinds informers
  • ReplicaSet algo: list Pods by selector.matches(pod.labels); classify owned (controller ownerRef.uid == rs.uid) / adoptable (no controller ref) / release (label no longer matches). Adopt → PATCH ownerRef (CAS on RV); orphan → remove ownerRef. diff = filtered_active.len() - spec.replicas: diff<0 → create |diff| Pods from template (deterministic generateName, ownerRef{controller:true, blockOwnerDeletion:true}); diff>0 → delete diff Pods ranked by deletion cost (Pending > unscheduled > NotReady > newest > lower restart). Burst cap ≤500 creates/deletes per sync (SSAR-style); skip create if deletion_timestamp set.
  • GC algo: informers on all kinds feed OwnerGraph. On owner delete event → for each dependent: if owner gone & !orphaned → enqueue delete; foreground → owner keeps foregroundDeletion finalizer until all blockOwnerDeletion:true deps removed; background → delete owner first, sweep deps async; orphan → strip ownerRefs then delete owner. Stale-ownerRef ref counting; absent owner UID ⇒ delete dependent.
  • Node algo: lease.renewTime vs now; now - renew > leaseDuration*(monitorGracePeriod) → set Ready=False; after pod_eviction_timeout (40s) add taint + evict (delete Pods with node_name==node, except DaemonSet-owned/mirror). Eviction = Pod delete → ReplicaSet recreates.

5.3 Deliverable

orc-controller binary runs all three controllers under the framework + leader-election hook; orctl scale rs/<n> --replicas=K converges; orctl delete rs/<n> cascades to Pods; killing a node's Lease reschedules its Pods. Drives the live cluster from Phase 1–4.

5.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Scale RS 3→10: 7 Pods created, status.replicas==10, observedGeneration==spec.generation within 2s (no scheduler).
  • Scale 10→3: 7 deleted by deletion-cost order; idempotent re-reconcile creates/deletes 0.
  • Adopt: pre-existing matching label-only Pod gains controller ownerRef; count not double-incremented.
  • delete rs (background): RS gone immediately, all child Pods deleted ≤1s; (foreground): RS lingers w/ finalizer until last child gone.
  • Node Lease lost: Ready=False ≤ grace (default 40s), taint added, Pods evicted + recreated on live nodes.

5.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
rs_scale_up.yaml (replicas:5) 5 Pods, controller ownerRef.uid==rs.uid, status.replicas==5
rs_adopt_orphan.yaml orphan with matching labels adopted; label mismatch → released (ownerRef removed)
gc_cascade_foreground.yaml owner finalizer foregroundDeletion held until deps==0, then owner deleted
gc_orphan_policy.yaml propagationPolicy:Orphan → children survive, ownerRefs stripped
node_notready.json (lease stale) Ready=False event, taint node.orc/unreachable, eviction events
Malformed selector matching 0 Pods (no-op); two RS selectors overlapping a Pod → adoption conflict logged, no double-adopt; ownerRef cycle A→B→A → GC breaks cycle, no infinite loop

5.6 Pitfalls

  • Adoption race: two RS selectors match one Pod → both PATCH ownerRef → second 409s. Root: overlapping selectors. Debug: controller-ref conflict event; resolve by single-controller invariant (one controller:true ref max).
  • Stale cache over-create: reconcile from lagging informer before its own create lands → creates extra Pods. Mitigation: expectations tracking (UID-keyed expected creates/deletes; skip sync until satisfied or 5min TTL). Debug: replica oscillation in logs.
  • Orphan delete on transient miss: GC deletes child because owner missing from cache (not yet synced) → data loss. Root: acting before informers HasSynced. Fix: gate GC on all-informer sync + absentOwnerCache double-check via live GET.
  • Lease flap → eviction storm: brief network blip evicts whole node. Mitigation: grace period + rate-limited eviction (≤max 1 node's Pods/10s); secondary signal (apiserver still sees Node).
  • Finalizer deadlock: foreground finalizer never removed if a dep itself stuck deleting → owner stuck Terminating. Debug: kubectl get -o yaml finalizers; check dep's own finalizers.

5.7 Performance Concerns

  • Reconcile latency p99 < 20ms from cache (no live LIST per sync). 10k Pods / 1k RS: full resync sweep < 5s.
  • GC OwnerGraph is in-memory adjacency, O(1) per-uid lookup; graph mutations under single-writer lock; rebuild only on relist.
  • Batch create/delete via concurrent client calls (bounded semaphore, ≤50 in-flight); burst cap 500/sync prevents apiserver overload.
  • WorkQueue rate limiter (exponential 5ms→1000s) collapses hot RS into one reconcile; dedup by key.

5.8 Edge Cases

  • RS replicas:0 → delete all owned Pods, status.replicas==0 (valid, not deletion).
  • RS being deleted (deletion_timestamp set) → stop creating, let GC reap children.
  • Pod manually deleted → RS recreates (level-triggered); manual create matching selector → adopted, may exceed desired → deleted.
  • Node briefly NotReady then Ready before grace → no eviction, taint not added.
  • DaemonSet/mirror Pods on dead node → not evicted (owner recreates on same node / static).
  • ownerRef to nonexistent UID → GC treats as orphan-able; cycle → break by topological non-progress detection.

5.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Controller crash mid-create expectations TTL expires restart → relist → reconcile to desired (idempotent)
409 on Pod create/ownerRef PATCH CAS conflict re-read RV, retry via WorkQueue backoff
apiserver unavailable client error WorkQueue requeue w/ backoff; no local divergence (store is truth)
Split-brain (two leaders) leader-election lease lost fencing via leader lease; loser stops reconciling — see Phase 13
GC graph corruption dep count negative rebuild graph from relist; log + metric gc_graph_rebuild_total

5.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: replicaset_reconcile_duration, replicaset_expectations_pending, gc_attempt_to_delete_total, gc_dirty_queue_depth, node_lifecycle_evictions_total, per-controller workqueue_depth/retries.
  • Flags: --concurrent-rs-syncs=5, --node-monitor-grace=40s, --pod-eviction-timeout=40s, --terminated-pod-gc-threshold=12500.
  • Events: SuccessfulCreate/SuccessfulDelete (RS), NodeNotReady/TaintManagerEviction (node) — surfaced via orctl describe.
  • Leader election required (--leader-elect); only the leader's controllers reconcile to avoid duplicate Pod creation.
  • Logs: structured per reconcile (rs key, diff, expectations); GC logs graph deltas at debug.

17. Phase 6 — orc-scheduler: filter/score scheduling framework and binding

6.1 Objectives

  • Watch unscheduled Pods (spec.node_name == None, no deletion_timestamp) -> enqueue into SchedulingQueue (priority + backoff sub-queues).
  • One scheduling cycle per Pod: Filter (feasible Nodes) -> Score (rank 0..100 weighted) -> Bind (write spec.node_name).
  • Filters: PodFitsResources (Node allocatable - sum of bound pod requests), NodeSelector, Taints/Tolerations, required Affinity/AntiAffinity (requiredDuringScheduling).
  • Scores: LeastRequested, MostRequested, BalancedResourceAllocation, SpreadPriority (topology), preferred affinity. Weighted-sum, pick argmax (ties -> deterministic lowest node name).
  • Bind = exclusive, write-once (Txn CAS on node_name == None); no feasible Node -> mark Unschedulable + requeue with backoff. Preemption hook: trait wired, returns None (deferred to Phase 13).

6.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-17 (pluggable Filter/Score framework), ADR-18 (CAS bind exclusivity), ADR-15 (level-triggered; scheduler reads cache not events); INV-5 (Pod binds exactly once), INV-6 (scheduler sole writer of spec.node_name); P-3 (no distributed locks — CAS bind), P-6 (single-writer spec.node_name); §5.2 Kubernetes Scheduling Framework KEP-624, Omega paper (optimistic-CC bind, shared-state scheduler); §5.3 pkg/scheduler (plugin framework, SchedulingQueue shape).

Module (orc-scheduler/src/) Contents
queue.rs SchedulingQueue: active (BinaryHeap by priority then enqueue-seq), backoff (min-heap by retry-time), unschedulable map; add/pop/requeue(item,Duration); flush backoff->active on timer (1s).
framework.rs SchedulingContext<'a>{ pod, node_lister, snapshot, plugin_state }; plugin registry; runs Filter then Score; FilterResult{Fit, Unschedulable(reason)}.
plugins/filter.rs PodFitsResources, NodeSelectorFit, TaintToleration, NodeAffinity, InterPodAffinity — all impl FilterPlugin.
plugins/score.rs LeastRequested, MostRequested, BalancedResourceAllocation, SpreadPriority, PreferredAffinity — all impl ScorePlugin; per-plugin weight in config.
cache.rs NodeSnapshot: per-Node allocatable, requested (sum of requests of pods already bound, from informer cache), incrementally updated on Pod add/delete.
binder.rs Binding{pod: ObjectRef, node: String}; bind() PUT to apiserver binding subresource -> store Txn CAS.
main.rs orc-scheduler binary: leader-election hook (Phase 13), informers on Pod+Node, the schedule loop.
  • Informers (reuse orc-controller SharedIndexInformer) on Pod + Node feed NodeSnapshot; only assumed/bound pods count toward requested.
  • Schedule cycle: pop active -> snapshot Nodes -> parallel Filter (rayon-free, plain iter) -> if 0 feasible: Unschedulable; else weighted Score, argmax -> assume (optimistically credit NodeSnapshot) -> async bind(); on bind 409/error: un-assume + requeue backoff.

6.3 Deliverable

orc-scheduler binary: watches unscheduled Pods, binds each to a feasible Node honoring resources/selectors/taints/affinity; writes spec.node_name via apiserver CAS; emits Scheduled/FailedScheduling Events. Library exports FilterPlugin, ScorePlugin, SchedulingContext, Binding, SchedulingQueue.

6.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Pod with requests{cpu:500m,mem:256Mi} binds only to a Node with ≥ that allocatable - already-requested; over-committed Node filtered out.
  • nodeSelector / required nodeAffinity mismatch -> Node infeasible; matching taint w/o toleration -> infeasible.
  • No feasible Node -> Pod stays Pending, status.conditions[PodScheduled]=False reason=Unschedulable, requeued (not lost).
  • Bind is write-once: concurrent second bind -> 409, no overwrite; bound node_name immutable.
  • Scheduling throughput ≥ 500 pods/s (100-Node cluster, in-mem store); per-pod p99 latency < 20ms.

6.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
fit_basic.yaml (1 pod, 3 nodes, 1 fits by cpu) binds to the one fitting node
packing.yaml (5 pods, 2 nodes, LeastRequested) spread ~even; MostRequested cfg -> packed onto node-1 first
selector_taint.yaml binds only to labeled+tolerated node
unschedulable.yaml (requests > every allocatable) PodScheduled=False, reason Unschedulable, no bind
bind_race.yaml (2 schedulers, 1 pod) — malformed/adversarial exactly one bind, other gets 409
Malformed: requests > limits, negative request, unknown resource name rejected pre-schedule (validation), Pod never enters active queue

6.6 Pitfalls

  • Double-binding / race with self: not crediting NodeSnapshot on assume -> next pod sees stale free capacity -> overcommit. Fix: assume immediately, before async bind returns. Debug: log assumed vs committed delta per Node.
  • Bind succeeds, status lag: kubelet hasn't reported -> scheduler re-sees pod as bound via node_name set (level-triggered), not status. Filter on spec.node_name, never on phase.
  • Resource leak on un-assume: bind 409 but assume not reverted -> phantom usage. Always pair assume/un-assume in a guard.
  • Selector vs field-selector confusion: nodeSelector is match_labels on Node labels, not field selector. Mismatched key -> silently infeasible everywhere. Debug: dump per-node filter reasons.
  • Tie-break nondeterminism: argmax without stable tie-break -> flapping/test flakiness. Fix: tie -> lowest node name.
  • Compaction/410 on watch: scheduler informer relists; in-flight assumed pods must survive relist (keyed by uid).

6.7 Performance Concerns

  • Filter is O(nodes × plugins); cap per-cycle Node scan via percentageOfNodesToScore (default min(50%, 100)) once feasible-enough.
  • NodeSnapshot incremental (delta on informer events), not recomputed per cycle -> O(1) amortized capacity lookup.
  • Score only feasible Nodes; short-circuit when single feasible. Target alloc-free filter hot path (preallocated reason buffers).
  • Single-threaded cycle (1 pod at a time) keeps assume bookkeeping race-free; throughput from cycle speed, not parallelism.

6.8 Edge Cases

  • Pod already has node_name (statically scheduled / re-list) -> skip, never re-bind.
  • Pod deleted mid-cycle (deletion_timestamp set) -> abort before bind.
  • Zero Nodes / all NotReady -> Unschedulable, not panic; requeue.
  • Node deleted between filter and bind -> bind 409/404 -> un-assume + requeue.
  • Equal-score Nodes -> deterministic lowest-name pick (no RNG).
  • Pod with no resource requests -> fits any Node (requests default 0), scored by spread only.

6.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Effect Mitigation
Scheduler crash mid-cycle assumed-but-unbound pods stay Pending level-triggered: restart relists, re-schedules; no durable scheduler state
Apiserver bind 409 storm retries chew CPU exponential backoff in SchedulingQueue.backoff, cap 10s
Snapshot drift vs reality overcommit / starvation resync informer; kubelet rejects oversubscribed pod -> reschedule
All pods unschedulable queue grows unbounded unschedulable map (not active heap); flushed only on Node/Pod cluster events

6.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: scheduler_e2e_latency_seconds (queue->bind), pending_pods{queue}, schedule_attempts{result}, framework_extension_duration{plugin,phase}.
  • Config: KubeSchedulerConfiguration-like TOML — plugin enable/disable + per-Score weights + percentageOfNodesToScore.
  • Single active scheduler via leader election (Phase 13); standby schedulers idle. Multiple active -> bind CAS still safe but wasted work.
  • Debug: orctl get pod -o yaml shows PodScheduled condition + Events (FailedScheduling carries per-predicate reason counts, e.g. 3 Insufficient cpu, 1 node(s) had taint).

18. Phase 7 — orc-kubelet: node agent, pod lifecycle, status, node lease, probes

7.1 Objectives

  • Register Node (capacity/allocatable/conditions/taints) on startup; renew its orc-store Lease (TTL 10s, renew every 2.5s) as the liveness heartbeat consumed by the Node-lifecycle controller (Phase 5).
  • Watch Pods where spec.node_name == self (field selector); spawn one PodWorker per pod; converge each to desired phase via a level-triggered sync loop reading full pod state (never per-event).
  • Drive pod lifecycle: admit -> sandbox -> pull images -> create+start containers -> Running; honor RestartPolicy with crash backoff; terminate with grace period.
  • Kubelet is the SOLE writer of pod.status (single-writer invariant); report PodStatus + Node.status via optimistic-concurrency PATCH.
  • Run liveness/readiness/startup probes via ProbeManager: liveness fail -> restart container; readiness gates EndpointSlice membership; startup gates the other two until first success.
  • Drive orc-runtime only through the ContainerRuntime trait — stubbed this phase (in-memory fake; real OCI/runc in Phase 8).

7.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-19 (kubelet sole status writer), ADR-20 (ContainerRuntime trait seam), ADR-21 (out-of-proc container PIDs), ADR-27 (lease-based node heartbeat); INV-6 (kubelet sole pod.status writer); P-6 (single-writer per field); §5.2 Borg paper (per-node Borglet agent model), etcd Lease docs (keepalive semantics); §5.3 pkg/kubelet (PodWorker, StatusManager, probe manager, node lease).

Module (orc-kubelet) Adds
node.rs NodeStatusReporter: register Node (idempotent create-or-update), compute capacity (cpu millicores, memory bytes from /proc or stub), set_ready(), lease grant+keepalive task
pod_worker.rs PodWorker per-pod actor; enum PodState{Admitted,SandboxReady,ImagesPulled,ContainersRunning,Terminating,Terminated}; syncPod() idempotent transition fn; mailbox mpsc<SyncTrigger> (watch delta, probe result, container-exit, periodic 10s)
pod_manager.rs watch loop -> desired pod set; spawn/stop PodWorker; PodSyncResult{Synced(PodStatus)|Backoff(Duration)|Terminated}
probe.rs ProbeManager::probe(&Probe,&ContainerStatus)->ProbeResult; per-container probe workers (HTTP/TCP/Exec); thresholds (period, timeout, success/failure counts)
status.rs StatusManager: dedup channel, batch pod.status PATCH with read resourceVersion, 409->re-read-merge-retry; phase derivation from container statuses
volume.rs VolumeManager mount-point reconcile (emptyDir/projected stub; real in Phase 11)
  • Phase derivation: Pending (no container running) -> Running (≥1 started, none failed-terminal) -> Succeeded (all exit 0 & RestartPolicy!=Always) -> Failed (terminal nonzero & policy=Never) -> Unknown (runtime unreachable).
  • Crash backoff: per-container exponential 10s -> 20s -> 40s -> ... cap 300s, reset after 10 min healthy; PodSyncResult::Backoff requeues worker.
  • Termination: on deletion_timestamp, stop_container(grace=spec.terminationGracePeriod default 30s) -> SIGTERM, SIGKILL on expiry; teardown sandbox; then clear pod from status.

7.3 Deliverable

orc-kubelet binary: registers a Node + holds its lease, watches bound Pods, takes a bound Pod to Running against the stub ContainerRuntime, reports pod.status, and runs probes. orctl get pods -o wide shows the pod Running on the node; orctl get nodes shows Ready.

7.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
Bound Pod -> Running status visible < 2s (stub runtime)
Lease renewal jitter period 2.5s ±10%; miss -> NotReady within TTL 10s
Status PATCH on no-op resync zero writes (status unchanged -> skip PATCH)
Liveness fail -> container restart within 1 probe period after failureThreshold
Readiness false -> removed from EndpointSlice reflected ≤ 1 probe period
PodWorker count vs bound pods exactly equal; orphan workers GC'd ≤ 1s after pod delete

7.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expected
fixtures/kubelet/bound_pod.yaml -> phase transitions Pending->Running; container started; status PATCH sequence captured
fixtures/kubelet/liveness_fail.yaml probe 3x fail -> restart count increments; backoff applied
fixtures/kubelet/readiness_gate.yaml not-ready container -> Ready condition false, absent from endpoints
fixtures/kubelet/node_lease.golden lease grant + keepalive trace; expiry -> NotReady
fixtures/kubelet/grace_term.yaml SIGTERM at t0, SIGKILL at t=grace; status cleared
Malformed: bad_node_name.yaml (pod bound to other node) ignored — not in this kubelet's desired set
Malformed: nonexistent_image.yaml ImagePullBackOff reason, phase stays Pending, no crash

7.6 Pitfalls

  • Status write race / lost update: two PATCHes from concurrent goroutines clobber -> route ALL pod.status writes through single StatusManager channel; debug via PATCH audit log + resourceVersion deltas.
  • Lease keepalive starvation: blocked sync loop delays keepalive -> spurious NotReady eviction -> run lease task on a dedicated task, never inline with PodWorker; alert on keepalive latency > 1s.
  • Probe-during-startup false kill: liveness fires before app ready -> CrashLoop -> startup probe MUST gate liveness/readiness; check probe order in worker state.
  • Backoff not reset: flapping container resets timer wrong -> infinite fast restart or stuck max -> reset only after ≥10min continuous healthy; inspect restart_count + lastTransitionTime.
  • Stale PodWorker after rebind: pod deleted+recreated same name diff uid -> worker keyed by name acts on wrong pod -> key workers by uid, not name.
  • Phase flip-flop: deriving phase from in-flight runtime state mid-transition -> oscillation -> derive only from settled container statuses; debounce.

7.7 Performance Concerns

  • Target 110 pods/node; PodWorker ~per-pod task, idle CPU ~0; sync wakeups event-driven + 10s safety resync (not busy-poll).
  • Status PATCH coalescing: batch window 100ms, dedup by uid -> ≤ 10 PATCH/s/node under churn.
  • Single shared watch connection for all bound pods (not per-pod watch); probe workers share an HTTP client pool.
  • Node status report interval 10s, but PATCH only on diff (capacity static, conditions rare).

7.8 Edge Cases

  • Pod bound but image-pull fails forever -> ImagePullBackOff, never Running, lease unaffected.
  • Pod deletion_timestamp set while sandbox mid-creation -> abort creation, jump to Terminating.
  • Node lease lost then regained (transient store blip) -> kubelet does NOT re-register pods; controller re-marks Ready.
  • RestartPolicy=Never + exit 0 -> Succeeded, worker terminates, no restart.
  • Two containers, one Succeeded one Running, policy=Always -> pod stays Running.
  • Clock skew between kubelet and apiserver -> use store Revision/resourceVersion ordering, never wall-clock for status causality.

7.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Runtime unreachable status() err phase Unknown, retry w/ backoff, lease still renewed
apiserver down watch/PATCH err buffer status locally, retry; pods keep running (decoupled)
Lease keepalive fails keepalive err controller marks NotReady -> evicts pods after grace; kubelet retries grant
PodWorker panic task join err supervisor respawns worker from store state (idempotent resync)
Disk/status buffer full PATCH queue bound drop oldest no-op, keep latest-per-uid (last-write-wins on status)

7.10 Operational Notes

  • Flags: --node-name, --node-ip, --apiserver, --lease-ttl=10s, --max-pods=110, --runtime=stub.
  • Metrics: kubelet_pod_worker_duration_seconds, kubelet_lease_renew_latency, kubelet_pod_status_updates_total, kubelet_probe_results{result}, kubelet_running_pods.
  • Logs: per-pod by uid; PATCH audit (old/new resourceVersion); probe transition events.
  • Readiness self-check: kubelet sets own Node Ready only after runtime reachable + first pod sync; drains in-flight workers on SIGTERM before exit.

19. Phase 8 — orc-runtime: OCI images, runc-like container exec, cgroups/namespaces

8.1 Objectives

  • ImageStore: pull OCI image (Docker registry v2 / OCI distribution), verify manifest + layer digests (sha256), unpack layers into overlayfs rootfs; content-addressed, dedup by digest.
  • runc-like exec: build OciSpec, create Linux namespaces (pid/net/mnt/uts/ipc/user), apply cgroups v2 limits (cpu/memory/pids), bind mounts + pivot_root, exec entrypoint as PID 1 of pid-ns.
  • Pod Sandbox: shared net+ipc+uts namespace (pause-like infra container) the pod's containers join; per-container pid/mnt.
  • ContainerRuntime (CRI-like) impl wiring kubelet's PodWorker; restart policy + exponential crash backoff.
  • Stub runtime on non-Linux (cfg-gated) so CI + unit tests run cross-platform. NON-GOAL: image build, full registry auth flows beyond bearer/basic, GPU/devices.

8.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-20 (ContainerRuntime trait), ADR-21 (out-of-proc exec), ADR-22 (direct Linux syscalls), ADR-23 (cgroups v2 only), ADR-26 (secret tmpfs projection); INV-6 (kubelet sole writer, runtime owns exec); P-11 (node isolates untrusted workloads); §5.1 TLPI (clone flags, pivot_root, mount propagation, capabilities); §5.2 cgroups v2 docs, runc/libcontainer writeups; §5.3 opencontainers/runc (exec sequence), containerd (CRI surface, image pull); §5.4 OCI Runtime/Image/Distribution Specs, CRI, cgroups v2, Linux namespaces/clone(2).

Module (orc-runtime) Contents
image/store.rs ImageStore{root,layers,manifests}; pull_image(&ImageRef,Option<&Auth>)->ImageId; layer cache keyed by digest
image/registry.rs OCI distribution client: GET /v2/<repo>/manifests/<ref> (Accept manifest list+v2), token auth (WWW-Authenticate: Bearer), blob GET /v2/<repo>/blobs/<digest> streamed to temp, verify on close
image/unpack.rs gzip-decode + tar-extract each layer; apply whiteouts (.wh., .wh..wh..opq); assemble overlay lowerdir stack bottom->top
oci/spec.rs OciSpec{process,root,mounts,linux:{namespaces,resources,cgroupsPath}}; merge image config (Env/Entrypoint/Cmd/WorkingDir/User) + ContainerConfig
linux/namespaces.rs unshare(CLONE_NEWPID|NEWNS|NEWUTS|NEWIPC|NEWNET|NEWUSER); user-ns uid/gid map write before exec; setns to join sandbox net/ipc
linux/cgroup.rs CgroupHandle; write cpu.max,memory.max,memory.high,pids.max under /sys/fs/cgroup/orc.slice/<pod>/<ctr>; cgroup.procs enroll
linux/mount.rs mount overlay (lower/upper/work), mount /proc,/sys,/dev(tmpfs+devpts), bind volume mounts, pivot_root(new,old)+umount2(old,MNT_DETACH)
runtime/cri.rs ContainerRuntime impl: run_sandbox/pull_image/create_container/start_container/stop_container/status
runtime/backoff.rs per-container RestartBackoff: 10ms*2^n capped 5min, reset after 10min stable
stub.rs #[cfg(not(target_os="linux"))] no-op runtime returning synthetic ContainerStatus
  • Exec flow: clone3/fork+unshare -> child: set hostname (uts), mount-ns private, mount overlay, pivot_root, mount /proc, set cgroup (write cgroup.procs from parent pre-exec), drop caps, execvp(entrypoint). Parent records host PID for status/signals.
  • Sandbox: infra container holds net+ipc+uts ns (alive for pod lifetime); setup_pod_network (orc-net) runs against its netns; app containers setns join.
  • Stop: SIGTERM -> wait grace -> SIGKILL; reap via waitpid/pidfd; record exit code + OOMKilled (cgroup memory.events:oom_kill>0).

8.3 Deliverable

orc-runtime launches a real container from a pulled OCI image (Linux): verified digests, overlay rootfs, enforced cgroup limits, namespace isolation; wired into orc-kubelet PodWorker. Demo: orctl apply a busybox Pod -> Running with pod.status from kubelet. Stub runtime green on macOS CI.

8.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Image pull: manifest digest + every layer digest verified; tampered byte -> pull fails (no unpack). Re-pull of cached digest does zero network fetch.
  • memory.max=64Mi Pod that allocates 128Mi -> OOMKilled, exit 137, status reflects it; cpu.max throttles (measured <50% of one core at 50000 100000).
  • Namespace isolation: container ps sees only its tree (PID 1 = entrypoint); host PID differs; container hostname == pod name.
  • Sandbox shares net: two containers in one Pod reach each other on 127.0.0.1.
  • Crash loop: container exiting nonzero restarts with backoff sequence 10ms,20ms,…capped 5min; RestartPolicy::Never -> Failed, no restart.

8.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
oci/busybox-manifest.json + layer blobs parse, verify, unpack -> rootfs with /bin/sh
oci/bad-layer-digest.json pull -> RuntimeError::DigestMismatch, no partial rootfs
oci/whiteout-layer.tar upper layer .wh.foo deletes lower foo in merged view
spec/expected-ocispec.json OciSpec golden: ns set, cgroupsPath, mounts, merged Env/Entrypoint
cgroup/limit-64mi.yaml Pod container OOMKilled, exit 137 (Linux gate)
Malformed: truncated gzip layer, manifest mediaType mismatch, layer digest sha512: unsupported, pivot_root on non-mountpoint typed errors, no leaked mounts/cgroups

8.6 Pitfalls
